 \u003cp\u003e\u003cb\u003eOffer Construct:\u003c/b\u003e HSBC Taj cardholders will earn accelerated rewards on spends on select Apple product purchases via the \u003cb\u003eHSBC Unicorn portal\u003c/b\u003e.\u003c/p\u003e\u0026#10;\u0026#10;    \u003ctbody\u003e\u0026#10;      \u003ctr\u003e\u0026#10;        \u003ctd\u003e\u003cb\u003eHSBC Taj\u003c/b\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\u0026#10;        \u003ctd align=\"center\"\u003e14X (21 points per \u0026#8377;100)\u003c/td\u003e\u0026#10;  \u003ch2\u003eTerms and Conditions\u003c/h2\u003e\u0026#10;  \u003col\u003e\u0026#10;    \u003cli\u003eThis Offer is valid till September 30, 2026, 11:59 PM. Accelerated Taj Reward points will be credited to the HSBC Taj Credit Card wallet within 60 days of completing the transaction.\u003c/li\u003e\u0026#10;    \u003cli\u003eThis Offer is applicable only when purchases are completed via the HSBC Unicorn Portal. The Offer is not applicable for purchases made directly at the Unicorn website or any of Unicorn's offline store.\u003c/li\u003e\u0026#10;    \u003cli\u003eThere shall be a capping of 20,000 accelerated reward points per month (cumulatively for all purchases via HSBC Unicorn Portal).\u003c/li\u003e\u0026#10;    \u003cli\u003eRewards point capping is based on calendar month and transaction settlement date will be considered for rewards capping. For example - if a customer completed transaction on 31 July 2026 and settlement happens on 1 August 2026, capping of 20,000 points will be considered for August 2026 and not for July 2026.\u003c/li\u003e\u0026#10;    \u003cli\u003eThe accelerated reward points shall be given on FullSwipe transactions only.\u003c/li\u003e\u0026#10;    \u003cli\u003eThe Offer is not applicable for IEMI/EMI transactions.\u003c/li\u003e\u0026#10;    \u003cli\u003eThis is applicable on select Apple Products (list given below). The product shall be delivered within 10 days from the date of successful transactions.\u003c/li\u003e\u0026#10;    \u003cli\u003eAll eligible products will be made available on the best effort basis. Policy","slug":"privacy-policy","content":"\u003cp\u003e\u003cbr\u003e\u003c/p\u003e  \u003ch1\u003ePrivacy Policy\u003c/h1\u003e  \u003cp\u003e\u003cfont size=\"4\"\u003eThis privacy policy sets out how Unicorn Infosolutions Pvt Ltd uses and protects any information that you give Unicorn Infosolutions Pvt Ltd when you use this website.\u003c/font\u003e\u003c/p\u003e  \u003ctable border=\"0\" cellpadding=\"20\" cellspacing=\"0\" width=\"100%\"\u003e \u003ctbody\u003e \u003ctr\u003e   \u003ctd valign=\"middle\" bgcolor=\"#f5f5f7\"\u003e     \u003cb\u003e\u003cfont size=\"4\"\u003eWe will not sell, distribute or lease your personal information to third parties unless we have your permission or are required by law to do so\u003c/font\u003e\u003c/b\u003e   \u003c/td\u003e \u003c/tr\u003e \u003c/tbody\u003e \u003c/table\u003e  \u003cbr\u003e  \u003cp\u003eUnicorn Infosolutions Pvt Ltd is committed to ensuring that your privacy is protected. Should we ask you to provide certain information by which you can be identified when using this website, then you can be assured that it will only be used in accordance with this privacy statement.\u003c/p\u003e  \u003cp\u003eUnicorn Infosolutions Pvt Ltd may change this policy from time to time by updating this page. You should check this page from time to time to ensure that you are happy with any changes.\u003c/p\u003e  \u003cbr\u003e  \u003chr\u003e  \u003ch2\u003eWhat We Collect\u003c/h2\u003e  \u003cp\u003eWe may collect the following information:\u003c/p\u003e  \u003ctable border=\"0\" cellpadding=\"20\" cellspacing=\"0\" width=\"100%\"\u003e \u003ctbody\u003e \u003ctr\u003e   \u003ctd valign=\"top\" bgcolor=\"#f5f5f7\"\u003e     \u003cb\u003e\u003cfont size=\"2\" color=\"#6b6b73\"\u003eINFORMATION WE MAY COLLECT\u003c/font\u003e\u003c/b\u003e     \u003cul\u003e     \u003cli\u003eName and job title.\u003c/li\u003e     \u003cli\u003eContact information including email address.\u003c/li\u003e     \u003cli\u003eDemographic information such as postcode, preferences and interests.\u003c/li\u003e     \u003cli\u003eOther information relevant to customer surveys and/or offers.\u003c/li\u003e     \u003c/ul\u003e   \u003c/td\u003e \u003c/tr\u003e \u003c/tbody\u003e \u003c/table\u003e  \u003cbr\u003e  \u003chr\u003e  \u003ch2\u003eWhat We Do With The Information We Gather\u003c/h2\u003e  \u003cp\u003eWe require this information to understand your needs and provide you with a better service, and in particular for the following reasons:\u003c/p\u003e  \u003cul\u003e \u003cli\u003eInternal record keeping.\u003c/li\u003e \u003cli\u003eWe may use the information to improve our products and services.\u003c/li\u003e \u003cli\u003eWe may periodically send promotional emails/SMS/RCS about new products, special offers or other information which we think you may find interesting using the email address which you have provided.\u003c/li\u003e \u003cli\u003eFrom time to time, we may also use your information to contact you for market research purposes. We may contact you by email, phone, fax or mail.\u003c/li\u003e \u003cli\u003eWe may use the information to customise the website according to your interests.\u003c/li\u003e \u003c/ul\u003e  \u003cbr\u003e  \u003chr\u003e  \u003ch2\u003eSecurity\u003c/h2\u003e  \u003cp\u003eWe are committed to ensuring that your information is secure. In order to prevent unauthorised access or disclosure we have put in place suitable physical, electronic and managerial procedures to safeguard and secure the information we collect online.\u003c/p\u003e  \u003cbr\u003e  \u003chr\u003e  \u003ch2\u003eHow We Use Cookies\u003c/h2\u003e  \u003cp\u003eA cookie is a small file which asks permission to be placed on your computer\u0026#8217;s hard drive. Once you agree, the file is added and the cookie helps analyse web traffic or lets you know when you visit a particular site. Cookies allow web applications to respond to you as an individual. The web application can tailor its operations to your needs, likes and dislikes by gathering and remembering information about your preferences.\u003c/p\u003e  \u003cp\u003eWe use traffic log cookies to identify which pages are being used. This helps us analyse data about webpage traffic and improve our website in order to tailor it to customer needs. We only use this information for statistical analysis purposes and then the data is removed from the system.\u003c/p\u003e  \u003cp\u003eOverall, cookies help us provide you with a better website, by enabling us to monitor which pages you find useful and which you do not. A cookie in no way gives us access to your computer or any information about you, other than the data you choose to share with us.\u003c/p\u003e  \u003cp\u003eYou can choose to accept or decline cookies. Most web browsers automatically accept cookies, but you can usually modify your browser setting to decline cookies if you prefer. This may prevent you from taking full advantage of the website.\u003c/p\u003e  \u003cbr\u003e  \u003chr\u003e  \u003ch2\u003eLinks To Other Websites\u003c/h2\u003e  \u003cp\u003eOur website may contain links to other websites of interest. However, once you have used these links to leave our site, you should note that we do not have any control over that other website. Therefore, we cannot be responsible for the protection and privacy of any information which you provide whilst visiting such sites and such sites are not governed by this privacy statement. You should check this page from time to time to ensure that you are happy with any changes. This policy is effective from 1st January 2025.\u003c/p\u003e\u0026#10;\u0026#10;\u003cbr\u003e\u0026#10;\u003chr\u003e\u0026#10;\u0026#10;\u003ch2\u003eWhat We Collect\u003c/h2\u003e\u0026#10;\u0026#10;\u003cp\u003eWe may collect the following information:\u003c/p\u003e\u0026#10;\u0026#10;\u003ctable border=\"0\" cellpadding=\"20\" cellspacing=\"0\" width=\"100%\"\u003e\u003ctbody\u003e\u003ctr\u003e\u003ctd valign=\"top\" bgcolor=\"#f5f5f7\"\u003e\u003cb\u003e\u003cfont size=\"2\" color=\"#6b6b73\"\u003eINFORMATION WE MAY COLLECT\u003c/font\u003e\u003c/b\u003e\u003cul\u003e\u003cli\u003eName and job title.\u003c/li\u003e\u003cli\u003eContact information including email address.\u003c/li\u003e\u003cli\u003eDemographic information such as postcode, preferences and interests.\u003c/li\u003e\u003cli\u003eOther information relevant to customer surveys and/or offers.\u003c/li\u003e\u003c/ul\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\u003c/tr\u003e\u003c/tbody\u003e\u003c/table\u003e\u0026#10;\u0026#10;\u003cbr\u003e\u0026#10;\u003chr\u003e\u0026#10;\u0026#10;\u003ch2\u003eWhat We Do With The Information We Gather\u003c/h2\u003e\u0026#10;\u0026#10;\u003cp\u003eWe require this information to understand your needs and provide you with a better service, and in particular for the following reasons:\u003c/p\u003e\u0026#10;\u0026#10;\u003cul\u003e\u0026#10;\u003cli\u003eInternal record keeping.\u003c/li\u003e\u0026#10;\u003cli\u003eWe may use the information to improve our products and services.\u003c/li\u003e\u0026#10;\u003cli\u003eWe may periodically send promotional emails/SMS/RCS about new products, special offers or other information which we think you may find interesting using the email address which you have provided.\u003c/li\u003e\u0026#10;\u003cli\u003eFrom time to time, we may also use your information to contact you for market research purposes. We may contact you by email, phone, fax or mail.\u003c/li\u003e\u0026#10;\u003cli\u003eWe may use the information to customise the website according to your interests.\u003c/li\u003e\u0026#10;\u003c/ul\u003e\u0026#10;\u0026#10;\u003cbr\u003e\u0026#10;\u003chr\u003e\u0026#10;\u0026#10;\u003ch2\u003eSecurity\u003c/h2\u003e\u0026#10;\u0026#10;\u003cp\u003eWe are committed to ensuring that your information is secure. In order to prevent unauthorised access or disclosure we have put in place suitable physical, electronic and managerial procedures to safeguard and secure the information we collect online.\u003c/p\u003e\u0026#10;\u0026#10;\u003cbr\u003e\u0026#10;\u003chr\u003e\u0026#10;\u0026#10;\u003ch2\u003eHow We Use Cookies\u003c/h2\u003e\u0026#10;\u0026#10;\u003cp\u003eA cookie is a small file which asks permission to be placed on your computer\u0026#8217;s hard drive. Once you agree, the file is added and the cookie helps analyse web traffic or lets you know when you visit a particular site. Cookies allow web applications to respond to you as an individual. The web application can tailor its operations to your needs, likes and dislikes by gathering and remembering information about your preferences.\u003c/p\u003e\u0026#10;\u0026#10;\u003cp\u003eWe use traffic log cookies to identify which pages are being used. This helps us analyse data about webpage traffic and improve our website in order to tailor it to customer needs. We only use this information for statistical analysis purposes and then the data is removed from the system.\u003c/p\u003e\u0026#10;\u0026#10;\u003cp\u003eOverall, cookies help us provide you with a better website, by enabling us to monitor which pages you find useful and which you do not. A cookie in no way gives us access to your computer or any information about you, other than the data you choose to share with us.\u003c/p\u003e\u0026#10;\u0026#10;\u003cp\u003eYou can choose to accept or decline cookies. Most web browsers automatically accept cookies, but you can usually modify your browser setting to decline cookies if you prefer. This may prevent you from taking full advantage of the website.\u003c/p\u003e\u0026#10;\u0026#10;\u003cbr\u003e\u0026#10;\u003chr\u003e\u0026#10;\u0026#10;\u003ch2\u003eLinks To Other Websites\u003c/h2\u003e\u0026#10;\u0026#10;\u003cp\u003eOur website may contain links to other websites of interest. However, once you have used these links to leave our site, you should note that we do not have any control over that other website. Therefore, we cannot be responsible for the protection and privacy of any information which you provide whilst visiting such sites and such sites are not governed by this privacy statement. You should exercise caution and look at the privacy statement applicable to the website in question.\u003c/p\u003e\u0026#10;\u0026#10;\u003cbr\u003e\u0026#10;\u003chr\u003e\u0026#10;\u0026#10;\u003ch2\u003eHSBC Offer \u0026#8212; Information Sharing\u003c/h2\u003e\u0026#10;\u0026#10;\u003ctable border=\"0\" cellpadding=\"20\" cellspacing=\"0\" width=\"100%\"\u003e\u003ctbody\u003e\u003ctr\u003e\u003ctd valign=\"top\" bgcolor=\"#f5f5f7\"\u003e\u003cb\u003e\u003cfont size=\"2\" color=\"#6b6b73\"\u003eCONSENT TO SHARE WITH HSBC\u003c/font\u003e\u003c/b\u003e\u003cp\u003eBy participating in the HSBC \u0026amp; Unicorn Infosolutions Pvt Ltd offer, customers consent to sharing transaction-related information between HSBC and Unicorn Infosolutions Pvt Ltd solely for offer validation, fraud prevention, reward processing, and compliance purposes.\u003c/p\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\u003c/tr\u003e\u003c/tbody\u003e\u003c/table\u003e\u0026#10;\u0026#10;\u003cbr\u003e\u0026#10;\u003chr\u003e\u0026#10;\u0026#10;\u003ch2\u003ePersonal Information\u003c/h2\u003e\u0026#10;\u0026#10;\u003cp\u003eYou may choose to restrict the collection or use of your personal information in the following ways:\u003c/p\u003e\u0026#10;\u0026#10;\u003cul\u003e\u0026#10;\u003cli\u003eWhenever you are asked to fill in a form on the website, look for the box that you can click to indicate that you do not want the information to be used by anybody for direct marketing purposes.\u003c/li\u003e\u0026#10;\u003cli\u003eIf you have previously agreed to us using your personal information for direct marketing purposes, you may change your mind at any time by writing to or emailing us at \u003ca href=\"mailto:unicornsupport@unicornstore.in\"\u003eunicornsupport@unicornstore.in\u003c/a\u003e.\u003c/li\u003e\u0026#10;\u003c/ul\u003e\u0026#10;\u0026#10;\u003cp\u003eWe will not sell, distribute or lease your personal information to third parties unless we have your permission or are required by law to do so. We may use your personal information to send you promotional information about third parties which we think you may find interesting if you tell us that you wish this to happen.\u003c/p\u003e\u0026#10;\u0026#10;\u003cp\u003eYou may request details of personal information which we hold about you under the Data Protection Act 1998. A small fee will be payable. If you would like a copy of the information held on you please write to: Unicorn Infosolutions Pvt Ltd, B/9, Shivalik, Malviya Nagar, New Delhi-110017.\u003c/p\u003e\u0026#10;\u0026#10;\u003cp\u003eIf you believe that any information we are holding on you is incorrect or incomplete, please write to or email us as soon as possible, at the above address. We will promptly correct any information found to be incorrect.\u003c/p\u003e\u0026#10;\u0026#10;\u003cbr\u003e\u0026#10;\u003chr\u003e\u0026#10;\u0026#10;\u003ch2\u003eContacting Policy\u003c/h1\u003e\u0026#10;\u0026#10;\u003cp\u003e\u003cfont size=\"4\"\u003eUnicorn Infosolutions Pvt Ltd believes in helping its customers as far as possible and has, therefore, a liberal cancellation policy. Under this policy:\u003c/font\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u0026#10;\u0026#10;\u003ctable border=\"0\" cellpadding=\"20\" cellspacing=\"0\" width=\"100%\"\u003e\u0026#10;\u003ctbody\u003e\u0026#10;\u003ctr\u003e\u0026#10;  \u003ctd valign=\"middle\" bgcolor=\"#f5f5f7\"\u003e\u0026#10;    \u003cb\u003e\u003cfont size=\"4\"\u003eAll order cancellations are subject to 3% cancellation fee\u003c/font\u003e\u003c/b\u003e\u0026#10;  \u003c/td\u003e\u0026#10;\u003c/tr\u003e\u0026#10;\u003c/tbody\u003e\u0026#10;\u003c/table\u003e\u0026#10;\u0026#10;\u003cbr\u003e\u0026#10;\u0026#10;\u003cul\u003e\u0026#10;\u003cli\u003eThere is no cancellation of orders placed under the Same Day Delivery category.\u003c/li\u003e\u0026#10;\u003cli\u003eNo cancellations are entertained for those products that the Unicorn Infosolutions Pvt Ltd marketing team has obtained on special occasions like Pongal, Diwali, and Valentine\u0026#8217;s Day etc. These are limited occasion offers and therefore cancellations are not possible.\u003c/li\u003e\u0026#10;\u003cli\u003eNo Cancellation allowed under affordability program. Product once booked availing cashback offers and/or EMI options cannot be cancelled, return or exchanged (with other variants of the same product) under any circumstances.\u003c/li\u003e\u0026#10;\u003cli\u003eUnicorn may levy cancellation fees on orders flagged as retailer/reseller transactions, especially where cancellations occur after the free window, at delivery, or show abnormal patterns. The fee (fixed 3% or a percentage of the order value) will be recovered from refunds or payment methods and is generally non-refundable.\u003c/li\u003e\u0026#10;\u003c/ul\u003e\u0026#10;\u0026#10;\u003cbr\u003e\u0026#10;\u0026#10;\u003chr\u003e\u0026#10;\u0026#10;\u003ch2\u003eReturn \u0026amp; Refund Policy\u003c/h2\u003e\u0026#10;\u0026#10;\u003cp\u003eThere could be certain circumstances beyond our control where you could receive a damaged / defective product or a product that is not the same as per your original order. We will replace the product to your satisfaction at no extra cost. In such circumstances, before using the product, please get in touch with our Customer Service Team who will guide you on the process for the same at our Customer Service number @ \u003ca href=\"tel:02267354000\"\u003e022 6735 4000\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u0026#10;\u0026#10;\u003cp\u003eThe return process of the product can be restricted depending on the nature and category of the product.\u003c/p\u003e\u0026#10;\u0026#10;\u003cbr\u003e\u0026#10;\u0026#10;\u003ctable border=\"0\" cellpadding=\"20\" cellspacing=\"0\" width=\"100%\"\u003e\u0026#10;\u003ctbody\u003e\u0026#10;\u003ctr\u003e\u0026#10;  \u003ctd valign=\"top\" bgcolor=\"#f5f5f7\"\u003e\u0026#10;    \u003cb\u003e\u003cfont size=\"2\" color=\"#6b6b73\"\u003eCONDITIONS FOR RETURN\u003c/font\u003e\u003c/b\u003e\u0026#10;    \u003cul\u003e\u0026#10;    \u003cli\u003ePlease notify us of receipt of a Damaged / Defective product within maximum \u003cb\u003e24 hours\u003c/b\u003e of delivery.\u003c/li\u003e\u0026#10;    \u003cli\u003eA clear and \u003cb\u003euninterrupted unboxing video\u003c/b\u003e is mandatory as proof for any Damaged / Defective / Wrong product claim. The video must be recorded in a single take and should clearly show the sealed outer packaging with the shipping label intact, the complete opening of the package, and the damage / defect / mismatch of the product. Claims submitted without a valid unboxing video may not be accepted.\u003c/li\u003e\u0026#10;    \u003cli\u003eProducts/Items should be \u003cb\u003eUNUSED\u003c/b\u003e.\u003c/li\u003e\u0026#10;    \u003cli\u003eProducts should be returned in their original packaging along with the original price tags, labels and invoices.\u003c/li\u003e\u0026#10;    \u003cli\u003eIt is advised that the return packets should be strongly and adequately packaged so that there is no further damage of goods in transit.\u003c/li\u003e\u0026#10;    \u003c/ul\u003e\u0026#10;  \u003c/td\u003e\u0026#10;\u003c/tr\u003e\u0026#10;\u003c/tbody\u003e\u0026#10;\u003c/table\u003e\u0026#10;\u0026#10;\u003cbr\u003e\u0026#10;\u0026#10;\u003chr\u003e\u0026#10;\u0026#10;\u003ch2\u003eRefunds\u003c/h2\u003e\u0026#10;\u0026#10;\u003cp\u003eWe will process the refund after receipt of the product by Unicorn Infosolutions Pvt Ltd or its business partner. Refund will be processed based on the mode of payment of the order.\u003c/p\u003e\u0026#10;\u0026#10;\u003ctable border=\"0\" cellpadding=\"16\" cellspacing=\"6\" width=\"100%\"\u003e\u0026#10;\u003ctbody\u003e\u0026#10;\u003ctr\u003e\u0026#10;  \u003ctd valign=\"top\" width=\"30%\" bgcolor=\"#f5f5f7\"\u003e\u003cb\u003eCredit / Debit card\u003c/b\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\u0026#10;  \u003ctd valign=\"top\" width=\"70%\" bgcolor=\"#f5f5f7\"\u003eRefunded by credit back to the credit / debit card within \u003cb\u003e7\u0026#8211;9 working days\u003c/b\u003e. Under this policy:\u003c/font\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u0026#10;\u0026#10;\u003ctable border=\"0\" cellpadding=\"20\" cellspacing=\"0\" width=\"100%\"\u003e\u0026#10;\u003ctbody\u003e\u0026#10;\u003ctr\u003e\u0026#10;  \u003ctd valign=\"middle\" bgcolor=\"#f5f5f7\"\u003e\u0026#10;    \u003cb\u003e\u003cfont size=\"4\"\u003eAll order cancellations are subject to 3% cancellation fee\u003c/font\u003e\u003c/b\u003e\u0026#10;  \u003c/td\u003e\u0026#10;\u003c/tr\u003e\u0026#10;\u003c/tbody\u003e\u0026#10;\u003c/table\u003e\u0026#10;\u0026#10;\u003cbr\u003e\u0026#10;\u0026#10;\u003cul\u003e\u0026#10;\u003cli\u003eThere is no cancellation of orders placed under the Same Day Delivery category.\u003c/li\u003e\u0026#10;\u003cli\u003eNo cancellations are entertained for those products that the Unicorn Infosolutions Pvt Ltd marketing team has obtained on special occasions like Pongal, Diwali, and Valentine\u0026#8217;s Day etc. These are limited occasion offers and therefore cancellations are not possible.\u003c/li\u003e\u0026#10;\u003cli\u003eNo Cancellation allowed under affordability program. Product once booked availing cashback offers and/or EMI options cannot be cancelled, return or exchanged (with other variants of the same product) under any circumstances.\u003c/li\u003e\u0026#10;\u003cli\u003eUnicorn may levy cancellation fees on orders flagged as retailer/reseller transactions, especially where cancellations occur after the free window, at delivery, or show abnormal patterns. The fee (fixed 3% or a percentage of the order value) will be recovered from refunds or payment methods and is generally non-refundable.\u003c/li\u003e\u0026#10;\u003c/ul\u003e\u0026#10;\u0026#10;\u003cbr\u003e\u0026#10;\u0026#10;\u003chr\u003e\u0026#10;\u0026#10;\u003ch2\u003eReturn \u0026amp; Refund Policy\u003c/h2\u003e\u0026#10;\u0026#10;\u003cp\u003eThere could be certain circumstances beyond our control where you could receive a damaged / defective product or a product that is not the same as per your original order. We will replace the product to your satisfaction at no extra cost. In such circumstances, before using the product, please get in touch with our Customer Service Team who will guide you on the process for the same at our Customer Service number @ \u003ca href=\"tel:02267354000\"\u003e022 6735 4000\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u0026#10;\u0026#10;\u003cp\u003eThe return process of the product can be restricted depending on the nature and category of the product.\u003c/p\u003e\u0026#10;\u0026#10;\u003cbr\u003e\u0026#10;\u0026#10;\u003ctable border=\"0\" cellpadding=\"20\" cellspacing=\"0\" width=\"100%\"\u003e\u0026#10;\u003ctbody\u003e\u0026#10;\u003ctr\u003e\u0026#10;  \u003ctd valign=\"top\" bgcolor=\"#f5f5f7\"\u003e\u0026#10;    \u003cb\u003e\u003cfont size=\"2\" color=\"#6b6b73\"\u003eCONDITIONS FOR RETURN\u003c/font\u003e\u003c/b\u003e\u0026#10;    \u003cul\u003e\u0026#10;    \u003cli\u003ePlease notify us of receipt of a Damaged / Defective product within maximum \u003cb\u003e24 hours\u003c/b\u003e of delivery.\u003c/li\u003e\u0026#10;    \u003cli\u003eA clear and \u003cb\u003euninterrupted unboxing video\u003c/b\u003e is mandatory as proof for any Damaged / Defective / Wrong product claim. The video must be recorded in a single take and should clearly show the sealed outer packaging with the shipping label intact, the complete opening of the package, and the damage / defect / mismatch of the product. Claims submitted without a valid unboxing video may not be accepted.\u003c/li\u003e\u0026#10;    \u003cli\u003eProducts/Items should be \u003cb\u003eUNUSED\u003c/b\u003e.\u003c/li\u003e\u0026#10;    \u003cli\u003eProducts should be returned in their original packaging along with the original price tags, labels and invoices.\u003c/li\u003e\u0026#10;    \u003cli\u003eIt is advised that the return packets should be strongly and adequately packaged so that there is no further damage of goods in transit.\u003c/li\u003e\u0026#10;    \u003c/ul\u003e\u0026#10;  \u003c/td\u003e\u0026#10;\u003c/tr\u003e\u0026#10;\u003c/tbody\u003e\u0026#10;\u003c/table\u003e\u0026#10;\u0026#10;\u003cbr\u003e\u0026#10;\u0026#10;\u003chr\u003e\u0026#10;\u0026#10;\u003ch2\u003eRefunds\u003c/h2\u003e\u0026#10;\u0026#10;\u003cp\u003eWe will process the refund after receipt of the product by Unicorn Infosolutions Pvt Ltd or its business partner. You should check this page from time to time to ensure that you are happy with any changes. This policy is effective from [Date].\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp class=\"MsoNormal\"\u003e\u003cb\u003eWhat we collect\u003c/b\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp class=\"MsoNormal\" align=\"justify\"\u003eWe may collect the following information:\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp class=\"15\"\u003e\u0026#8226; Name and job title\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp class=\"15\"\u003e\u0026#8226; Contact information including email address\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp class=\"15\"\u003e\u0026#8226; Demographic information such as postcode, preferences and interests\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp class=\"15\"\u003e\u0026#8226; Other information relevant to customer surveys and/or offers\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp class=\"MsoNormal\"\u003e\u003cb\u003eWhat we do with the information we gather\u003c/b\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp class=\"MsoNormal\" align=\"justify\"\u003eWe require this information to understand your needs and provide you with a better service, and in particular for the following reasons:\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp class=\"15\"\u003e\u0026#8226; Internal record keeping.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp class=\"15\"\u003e\u0026#8226; We may use the information to improve our products and services.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp class=\"15\"\u003e\u0026#8226; We may periodically send promotional emails/SMS/RCS about new products, special offers or other information which we think you may find interesting using the email address which you have provided.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp class=\"15\"\u003e\u0026#8226; From time to time, we may also use your information to contact you for market research purposes. We may contact you by email, phone, fax or mail.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp class=\"15\"\u003e\u0026#8226; We may use the information to customise the website according to your interests.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp class=\"MsoNormal\"\u003e\u003cb\u003eSecurity\u003c/b\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp class=\"MsoNormal\" align=\"justify\"\u003eWe are committed to ensuring that your information is secure. In order to prevent unauthorised access or disclosure we have put in place suitable physical, electronic and managerial procedures to safeguard and secure the information we collect online.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp class=\"MsoNormal\"\u003e\u003cb\u003eHow we use cookies\u003c/b\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp class=\"MsoNormal\" align=\"justify\"\u003eA cookie is a small file which asks permission to be placed on your computer\u0026#8217;s hard drive. Once you agree, the file is added and the cookie helps analyse web traffic or lets you know when you visit a particular site. Cookies allow web applications to respond to you as an individual. The web application can tailor its operations to your needs, likes and dislikes by gathering and remembering information about your preferences.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp class=\"MsoNormal\" align=\"justify\"\u003eWe use traffic log cookies to identify which pages are being used. This helps us analyse data about webpage traffic and improve our website in order to tailor it to customer needs. We only use this information for statistical analysis purposes and then the data is removed from the system.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp class=\"MsoNormal\" align=\"justify\"\u003eOverall, cookies help us provide you with a better website, by enabling us to monitor which pages you find useful and which you do not. A cookie in no way gives us access to your computer or any information about you, other than the data you choose to share with us.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp class=\"MsoNormal\" align=\"justify\"\u003eYou can choose to accept or decline cookies. Most web browsers automatically accept cookies, but you can usually modify your browser setting to decline cookies if you prefer. This may prevent you from taking full advantage of the website.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp class=\"MsoNormal\"\u003e\u003cb\u003eLinks to other websites\u003c/b\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp class=\"MsoNormal\" align=\"justify\"\u003eOur website may contain links to other websites of interest. However, once you have used these links to leave our site, you should note that we do not have any control over that other website. Therefore, we cannot be responsible for the protection and privacy of any information which you provide whilst visiting such sites and such sites are not governed by this privacy statement. You should exercise caution and look at the privacy statement applicable to the website in question.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp class=\"MsoNormal\"\u003e\u003cb\u003eControlling your personal information\u003c/b\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp class=\"MsoNormal\" align=\"justify\"\u003eYou may choose to restrict the collection or use of your personal information in the following ways:\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp class=\"15\"\u003e\u0026#8226; Whenever you are asked to fill in a form on the website, look for the box that you can click to indicate that you do not want the information to be used by anybody for direct marketing purposes.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp class=\"15\"\u003e\u0026#8226; If you have previously agreed to us using your personal information for direct marketing purposes, you may change your mind at any time by writing to or emailing us at unicornretail@uipl.co.in.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp class=\"MsoNormal\" align=\"justify\"\u003eWe will not sell, distribute or lease your personal information to third parties unless we have your permission or are required by law to do so. We may use your personal information to send you promotional information about third parties which we think you may find interesting if you tell us that you wish this to happen.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp class=\"MsoNormal\" align=\"justify\"\u003eYou may request details of personal information which we hold about you under the Data Protection Act 1998. A small fee will be payable.
